<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The user's vision is to create a dual-platform communication suite: AI KARAU, a webinar tool, and ENZI, a futuristic, AI-driven Actionable Intelligence professional messenger. This suite also includes MedMatch AI, a job-seeking toolkit. The application should feel like an active, predictive partner, not a static tool, and support a complex multi-domain strategy.

**PRODUCT REQUIREMENTS:**
1.  **Futuristic UI/UX:** Liquid Glass aesthetic, modular Bento Grid layouts, and a task-driven interface.
2.  **Intelligence & Behavior Learning:** Predictive navigation, context-aware triggers, and sentiment/tone analysis.
3.  **Advanced Writing Assistant:** An AI assistant for co-editing and message templating.
4.  **Expanded Authentication:** Support for multiple SSO providers and Passkeys.
5.  **Unified Workspace & Cross-Portal Integration:** Tightly bundle AI KARAU and ENZI, allowing side-by-side use. Meetings in KARAU should convert to persistent chat channels in ENZI.
6.  **Advanced Bot Ecosystem:** A comprehensive Bot Marketplace with specialized, functional bots.
7.  **Subdomain Architecture:** Support multiple domains routing to specific portals.
8.  **Multi-Platform Deployment:** The application must be ready for deployment on Web (PWA/TWA), Desktop (Windows/macOS via Electron), and Mobile (Android/iOS via Capacitor/Expo).
9.  **Microsoft Ecosystem Integration:** The application should integrate with Microsoft Teams and Outlook.
10. **Smart Apply Bot:** An AiApply-like bot for MedMatch that automatically finds and applies to jobs matching a user's resume, including generating tailored cover letters.

**User's preferred language**: English

**what currently exists?**
The application is a feature-rich, three-portal suite (MedMatch, AI KARAU, ENZI) that is stable and has been extensively tested. A comprehensive 4-phase testing strategy (Functional, Reliability, Regression, Deployment Readiness) was successfully executed with 185/185 tests passing, confirming the app's stability and readiness for all 8 target platforms.

Key components have been significantly refactored for maintainability, and new features like a CI/CD test pipeline and an email settings API have been added and verified. The user has provided a detailed, three-part recipe for the production launch, which has been documented in . The application is now at the pre-launch stage, pending execution of this external playbook.

**Upcoming and Future Tasks**
**Upcoming Tasks:**
These tasks are derived from the user's latest instructions and are detailed in . They are primarily external processes requiring guidance and validation rather than direct coding.

*   **P0 - Task 1: Submit Platform Builds per Wave Deployment Schedule:** Guide the user through building, signing, and submitting the iOS () and Android () release artifacts to App Store Connect and Google Play Console, following the wave-based rollout plan.
*   **P0 - Task 2: Register Azure AD for Microsoft Teams/Outlook Functionality:** Guide the user through creating an Azure AD app registration, configuring required Microsoft Graph API permissions (Mail, Calendar, Teams), granting admin consent, and securely storing the client secret.
*   **P0 - Task 3: Obtain CISO Security Sign-off (Gate G5):** Guide the user in assembling the security evidence pack (pen test reports, vulnerability scans), ensuring all critical/high findings are remediated, and scheduling the G5 review to get the final sign-off for production deployment. This is a hard blocker for Task 1.

**Future Tasks:**

*   **P2 - Production Monitoring & CRS Dashboard:** Build a dashboard for monitoring application health in production.
*   **P2 - Chrome Extension Implementation:** Develop a Chrome extension for the application.
*   **P3 - Enterprise Customer Onboarding Setup:** Create a streamlined process for onboarding enterprise customers.
